## Chapter 41
### Concerning the Three Blows that befell the Enemy because of the Light

*This is a remarkably clean substrate chapter — a compact, numbered three-fold eschatological teaching about three cosmic blows against the Enemy/Darkness. The entire structure (first war, second dissolution, third separation) maps cosmic process onto cosmic process with no contemporary application, no institutional content, no exhortation, and no citations. The only non-substrate material is the brief dialogue frame attribution in P1. Gardner's note about 'rudimentary redaction' in ch. 39-41 may refer to the sequence ordering rather than heavy editorial overlay within the chapter itself. The exhortation that Gardner mentions (exhorting the faithful despite persecution) is not present in the surviving text as given — it may have been in a lost section or may refer to the broader chapter context not included here. The oldest teaching layer is overwhelmingly dominant.*

The Darkness itself, the enemy, has received three bad blows with three wars; and worse dangers from the light in those three wars.

The first: (The enemy) was brought out to the middle and separated from his dark earth, whence he had departed. He was vanquished in that first war, caught and [bound] by the living soul.

The second blow is the time when he will be dissolved and melted, and he perishes in this great fire. He is destroyed and overthrown, from the images that are all by which he exists. And he will be gathered in [to the] fetter corresponding to his archetype, and be made like he was from the first beginning.

The third blow that will befall the enemy is the 'rolling back' [at the] end when everything will be separated, and male will be divided [from] female. Now, the male will be bound in the lump, but the female [will be thrown] in to the tomb. For it will be divided [ ... by a great] stone in their midst, for generation upon generation, for ever and ever.

⟨p.106⟩ This is the way that the binding of the enemy will come about, in a fetter that is burdensome and strong, one from which there is never escape; because [they have achieved] his binding and bound him for ever. And they have achieved his being set apart, and he has been set apart for ever.

**Temporal observations:**
- ¶1: Frame formula 'Once again he turned, and says:' is standard editorial attribution. The substantive content is a cosmological thesis statement that sets up the three-fold numbered teaching. Both sides (Darkness/enemy, light, three wars) are internal to the cosmic system. The thesis sentence defines the teaching structure and belongs to the oldest layer.
- ¶2: First enumeration unit. 'The first:' is the teaching structure marker. All terms are cosmic-systemic: 'the middle' (cosmic zone), 'dark earth' (realm of Darkness), 'living soul' (the captured light substance). Both sides of every mapping stay within the cosmic system. The 'living soul' is a personified cosmic entity — the light substance that binds the enemy in the first war.
- ¶3: Second enumeration unit. Pure cosmic-process vocabulary: 'dissolved and melted,' 'great fire,' 'images' (cosmic forms), 'fetter corresponding to his archetype,' 'first beginning.' The concept of being gathered into a fetter 'corresponding to his archetype' is deeply correspondential — the binding form mirrors the original cosmic form. This is eschatological substrate: describes what WILL happen within the cosmic system.
- ¶4: Third enumeration unit. 'Rolling back' is a cosmic-eschatological technical term. Male/female division describes the separation of cosmic principles, not gendered persons. 'Lump' (βώλος) and 'tomb' (τάφος) are cosmic repositories — both Greek loanwords in Coptic suggesting technical cosmological terminology transmitted through the tradition. The 'great stone' dividing them is a cosmic boundary marker. 'For generation upon generation, for ever and ever' describes the permanence of the cosmic state — no audience address or imperative voice. The mild exhortation score (4.41) appears driven by the 'for ever and ever' formula, not by actual exhortatory content.
- ¶5: Concluding summary of the three-blow teaching. Recapitulates the cosmic binding and separation described in the three enumerations. Both sides remain entirely within the cosmic system: fetter, binding, being set apart — all describe cosmic processes applied to the cosmic enemy. No audience address, no contemporary application, no institutional reference. The summary closure is architecturally complete: three blows enumerated, then their combined result stated.

## Chapter 42
### Concerning the Three Vessels

*The oldest teaching layer is strongly dominant. The chapter teaches the cosmic process of three-vessel discharge through two extended correspondential analogies (doctor/healing and mother/birth with body-layer enumeration) and a systematic twelve-deed enumeration. The analogies are structurally rich: the birth analogy maps seven body-layers (marrow, bones, tendons, flesh, veins, blood, skin) doubled through mother+child to eighteen garments — classic body-cosmos correspondence. The twelve deeds (¶14-32) are compact substrate enumeration of cosmic advantages. Editorial overlay is light: dialogue frame in ¶1-2 and ¶13, counterfactual contemporary applications in ¶16-20 (with 'today' anchors and one institutional reference to fasting in ¶19), and a closing self-reference in ¶33. The negative editorial fatigue score (-0.79) confirms the second half is cleaner than the first. Teaching purity 0.670. The seam flags on ¶17-18 are likely false positives — the bridge connectives continue a three-fold iteration (one per vessel) rather than extending to institutional content.*

these three vessels that [ ... ] and were discharged from above, and how they were discharged and separated from all the rulers who are arranged in the heavens. [...] the manner in which the three vessels have been discharged, while the rulers were flourishing in their body.

when these vessels were discharged, they were discharged in [ ... ] slaughter, but those [ ... ] these vessels from them.

⟨p.107⟩
They are like the form of a man who has fallen into a purulent sickness. Other wounds and diseases are in his body, and even bile and other poisons are in his inner limbs. He achieves good health by a wise doctor, who gives him one remedy to drink that causes his purulent sickness to break out above, and gives him another medicine to drink that causes his sickness to discharge below, and he gives him yet another medicine to drink that cures his wounds for him [ ... ] calmly and silently.

Through the skill of a wise doctor, with his effective medicines to consume that take away all sicknesses in this manner and dissolve all magic, the doctors shall [ ... ] they work cures through the aroma of the medicine, other wounds [ ... ] what is hidden, they at last tear them out from [ ... ] manifested above and below [ ... through] the aroma of edible medicines that were placed upon them.

This is also what these vessels of water, of darkness and of fire are like, which are hidden in the body of all the rulers above; while the Living Spirit is like the wise doctor. These three healing drugs are the three garments that the Living Spirit assumes upon his body: of wind, of water, and of fire. Through them he has set in order the things below. For by those three garments he has been displayed to the rulers who are above, with hard-heartedness, with shaking and fear, with derangement; from them he discharged the three vessels, those of water, of darkness and of fire.

The case of these vessels is just like a child who is in his mother's womb and is swathed by seven garments. The first is the marrow, the second is the bones, the third is the tendons, the fourth is the flesh, the fifth the veins, the sixth the blood, the seventh the skin of the body that covers him. The other garment also [ ... / ... in the womb of his mother. His mother also is just like] this child. The marrow and the bones, the sinews and flesh, the veins and blood and skin, together with the garment that she wears and the linen that swathes her, total nine. Together with the nine of the child, these and the others, they shall complete and make eighteen garments. Now, you shall find the soul of this child is entwined within all these.

⟨p.108⟩
And look see: When the smell of the action of the demon, which brings about the stench of birth, will reveal the child; immediately [ ... ] shall [ ... ] all [these things] and it emerges from a crack that has become visible, while no tear nor wound occurs in the body of the child. In the same way as this child opens up the womb of his mother, so is it also with these vessels of water, of darkness and of fire. The actions that open up his mother to the child, and she shakes him out and reveals him; these are the three living garments that the Living Spirit assumed on his body.

For by the aspect of the garment of water [ ... / ... ] from the rulers; he moved [ ... ] they are the poison and death with which their veins and entrails are filled.

By the aspect of the garment of wind, in which he was revealed [ ... ] he has swept away and erased the entire destructive shadow and dirt, discharged it down to the earth. Afterwards he swept it out and confined it in the vessel of darkness that surrounds the universe.

Also, by the garment of living fire, through its splendour and vigour, he discharged the fire of the darkness from all the rulers and threw it down upon this earth. He swept it another time from the earth and confined it in the vessel that surrounds all the worlds, and they call it the wall of great fire.

Now, those three garments, through which he established these three things, he did not peel them off all at once; but he stripped them off one by one at the appropriate time [ ... ] down to the earth [ ... ]

In this way the universe of the [ ... ... ] his vessels [ ... ] these vessels like [ ... ]

The use of the matter, and the management of these vessels that were swept out and discharged from above to earth, have brought an advantage and benefit of twelve great deeds. Afterwards, they were swept from the earth and pushed outside to the edges of the worlds and to the {extremity}.

The first deed is this: So that evil-doing does not increase in the universe, neither in the creations nor in the trees [ ... it has set i]tself up in three things.

The [first is / ...]

The second is error [ ... ... ] if [the vessel of water] had not been swept down from above, but rather the waters existing up till now in the rulers above [ ... / ... the] torrents that would flow [ ... ] and the droplets that would [ ... ] down, so lust would have sprouted [ ... i]n the trees and in the fruits as well as all flesh

if the vessel of darkness had not been swept out and thrown down to the earth, nor swept from the earth and cast outside the region of the worlds into this pit; again the drops of darkness, which will drip down from it, and the torrents that will continue to flow down from above to the earth and assume the trees and all flesh, would have multiplied

if the fire were in the rulers of the worlds of the heavens, and had not been swept out and cast down to the earth, and swept from the earth and shut up inside the vessel of fire that surrounds the worlds; [ ... ] this fire [ ... ... ] above through the torrent [ ... the torrent ... ] below;

and the dark fire would have multiplied in the tree and in fruit and in all flesh.

this single evil that was purified by three archetypes [ ... / ... ]

The second deed: Al[l] the heavens [ ... / ... ] of the sediment and the pollution [ ... ... ] so that [ ... / ... ] surpasses in them [ ... ]

[The third]: The heavens and the firmaments [ ... / ... b]urden [ ... ] relieved from the weight-carriers [ ... ] and it ceased being heavy. This rich one [ ... ... ] above.

The fourth: That they might purify and cleanse their dwelling places [ ... / ... ]

The fifth: Because the greatness and the richness that dwells in these ships, as it [ ... ] you might find them living in these ships, purified and holy.

The sixth: That you might find the mixed, incomplete, and unconfined temples above at rest and purified; since rest will reach them and pass through them and purify them.

The seventh: [ ... ] nature alone of the vessels, which will advance and extend to their places and sites [ ... / ... ] in [ ... ]

The eighth: That great enclosures and walls would be made, and confine everything.

The ninth: When the apostles [ ... ] them, they shall return to the universe and give a sign of [ ... ] the dissolution of the universe.

The tenth deed: That they would become releasers and purifiers of everything at the end of the universe like the fire [ ... / ... ] burns [ ... ]

The eleventh deed: [ ... ] will make a crossing [ ... ] and the entire ruling-power [ ... ]

⟨p.111⟩
to the outer land, for they were not envious of this [ ... ] a man who is richer than them; because the aeons of light are outside [ ... ] the place [ ... ] of peace, the place of honour and grace, the place where the joy of the householders who dwell in it shall never pass away nor recede.

The twelfth: That you might find all the deeds of loss hidden from the aeons of light, because of the wall of fire that surrounds the entire building; and these unclean things are displayed (only) in its [ ... ]

## Chapter 43
### Concerning the Vessels

*A remarkably clean teaching chapter dominated by substrate cosmological process description. The chapter describes the three-fold discharge cycle: the Father of Life pours dark vessels (water, darkness, fire) onto earth, the corresponding dark elements in the earths are drawn upward by sympathetic attraction ('like draws like'), then both are swept into prepared pits. The systematic parallel structure across paragraphs 9-11 (water, then darkness, then fire) is classic substrate enumeration. No editorial bridge, institutional, or exhortation material detected. The only later material is dialogue frame formulas in paragraphs 1, 2, 7, 8. The term 'Father of Life' for the demiurge (rather than 'Living Spirit') may preserve an earlier stratum as Gardner notes. The negative editorial fatigue score confirms the chapter stays consistently substratic throughout.*

When the Father of Life discharged these three vessels from above, they of water and of darkness and of fire, did he perhaps discharge them to their pits at the edge of the firmament? Or did he discharge them first to this earth, and then he swept them from the earth and threw them to their places?

when the Father of Life was about to discharge these three vessels he poured the three upon this earth, one by one at its (appointed) hour and season. He discharged them and cast them down to the earth.

Then he bailed and scooped them from this earth in these three garments, they of wind and of water and of fire, which swathed his body. He poured and made them flow into the three pits [ ... w]hich he had [es]tablished during the construc[tio]n of the worlds [ ... ] they [ ... ] something remained behind on the face of the earth from these three vessels, so that you can find their mark and type displayed in the universe.

The element that is left in water is the pungency and the salinity of this salty inner sea that surrounds the worlds.

And the remnant that is left from the darkness is this black mountain that exists in the surroundings of the sunrise. For when the sun would shine over the world no sunlight is visible there, because the darkness that gushes from it manifests above it.

⟨p.118⟩
And the remnant that remains behind from the fire, upon the earth, are all the mountains and islands from where fumes of fire gush up and are visible. For these things are the remnant of the great vessel, the vestige that was conjoined with this fire that comes up from the earth; together with the mountains and hills [ ... ] places where no fruit sprouts on the trees [ ... ] greater than that place from the limb and [ ... / ... ] the trees shall not bear fruit in that place.

about the vessels that surround the universe, and why they were not discharged from above into the ditches prepared for them (straightaway); but were firstly poured upon the earth, and afterwards were swept from the earth and cast down into their pits?

When the Father of Life discharged these vessels from above upon the earth, he poured them out for this reason: so that also the water and the darkness and the fire that exist in these three earths would be drawn and ascend towards they that had come down from above, and the earths would be purified and cleansed. Due to this he poured these vessels down to the earth, of water and of darkness and of fire, so that also the water and darkness and fire that exist in the earths would be drawn up towards they that had descended from above.

⟨p.113⟩
Now, first of all, he discharged the water, the waste, the poison of the rulers who are above. He cleansed the firmaments of the [water ... ] he poured down the dark waters. [Just as the] waters fell from heaven to earth, so also the waters below in the earths were drawn and came up from below towards the waters that had come from above to the earths. The waters [were] purified of the dark poison that exists in them. Then he swept them outside and made them a great vessel and a wall of sea that surrounds the universe, and on which the ships sail.

After the water, he discharged the darkness. He purified the firmaments and dried from them the darkness with which they are weighed down. He discharged it down to the earth. Just as he had purified the firmaments, so also he purified the earths. The darkness that is in the earths was drawn up towards the darkness that had been discharged from above. He swept them both out and shut them in that [ ... ] of darkness [ ... / ... ] the water.

And he discharged the fire from heaven to earth, [ ... at] that time again the fire that was shut [in] the earth below was drawn and came up towards its kind. Fire was added to fire. Afterwards, he swept it out and cast it into the outer underworld, which [he had constructed for] them outside the universe.

In the same way that he purified the firmaments above as a place for the light-givers to journey [ ... / ... ] so also he purified the earths below and swept from them the fire and the darkness and the water [ ... ] He has purified them for the ascent of these three powers, they of the wind and the water and the fire, which ascend before the Porter and traverse the earths.

**Temporal observations:**
- ¶1: The question is fully substantive — it defines the teaching structure by posing the exact cosmological process question (direct discharge vs. pour-then-sweep). This is the Persian pedagogical question format defining the topic.
- ¶2: The frame formula 'Then the apostle says to them' and the pedagogical transition 'As for what you have asked me... you must understand this, that' are editorial. The teaching content describes the sequential discharge process with temporal ordering ('one by one at its appointed hour and season').
- ¶3: Pure cosmological process. The three light garments (wind, water, fire) correspond to the three dark vessels — a systematic pairing. 'Mark and type displayed in the universe' is correspondential language: the remnants of cosmic process leave visible traces in the physical world. Both sides of the mapping stay within the cosmic system.
- ¶4: First of three remnant-correspondences. Maps residual dark water → sensory quality (pungency, salinity) → cosmic geography (inner sea surrounding worlds). Classic substrate: domain mapped onto domain, both sides within the cosmic system.
- ¶5: Second remnant-correspondence. Maps residual darkness → cosmic geography (black mountain near sunrise) → cosmological effect (blocks sunlight). Both sides internal to the cosmic system. The 'black mountain at sunrise' is cosmic geography, not a reference to any earthly location.
- ¶6: Third remnant-correspondence. Maps residual fire → cosmic geography (volcanic mountains, barren islands) → effects (fumes, barrenness). Significant lacunae in the second half. The page marker ⟨p.118⟩ suggests manuscript disruption. Both sides of mapping remain within the cosmic system — volcanic phenomena as traces of the cosmic fire vessel.
- ¶7: The substantive question defines the second teaching sequence — it asks WHY the indirect discharge method was used. This is the structural question that the paragraphs 8-12 answer. The frame formula and honorific address ('our master') are editorial.
- ¶8: The teaching describes the sympathetic attraction principle — like draws like across cosmic levels. Dark water from above draws dark water from below; dark fire draws dark fire. This is the core cosmological mechanism: discharge-attraction-purification. The repetitive phrasing (water/darkness/fire repeated twice) suggests oral-formulaic teaching structure.
- ¶9: First of three parallel discharge-purification sequences (water → darkness → fire). Describes the complete cycle: discharge from firmaments, sympathetic attraction of dark waters from the earths, purification, and formation into cosmic geography (wall of sea surrounding the universe). 'Poison of the rulers' is cosmic-agent vocabulary. 'Ships sail' refers to cosmic vessels (sun/moon ships) traversing the purified sea.
- ¶10: Second parallel discharge-purification sequence: darkness. Same structure as paragraph 9. The sympathetic attraction principle operates identically: darkness from below is drawn toward darkness discharged from above. Lacunae in the final portion obscure where exactly the darkness is confined.
- ¶11: Third parallel discharge-purification sequence: fire. 'Drawn and came up towards its kind' — the sympathetic attraction formula stated most concisely. 'Fire was added to fire' is the simplest expression of the like-draws-like principle. The fire is cast into the 'outer underworld' — cosmic geography. The three-fold parallel (water/darkness/fire) across paragraphs 9-11 is classic substrate enumeration structure.
- ¶12: Summary and purpose statement for the entire process. The purification creates pathways: above for the light-givers (sun, moon), below for the three light powers (wind, water, fire). 'The Porter' is a cosmic figure (one of the sons of the Living Spirit in Manichaean cosmology). Both sides of the mapping stay within the cosmic system: purified zones → pathways for cosmic beings. The light triad (wind, water, fire) mirrors the dark triad (water, darkness, fire) that was discharged.

## Chapter 44
### Concerning the Sea Giant

*This chapter is nearly pure substrate — systematic cosmological teaching about the sea giant's formation and cosmic function. Teaching purity 0.936 is accurate. No editorial bridge, institutional, or exhortation vocabulary detected. The only non-substrate material is the single-line dialogue frame formula in paragraph 1. The teaching describes: (1) how the Living Spirit directed waste from the three vessels into the sea, explaining its salt and bitterness; (2) how the Father of Life purified the stellar sphere, casting sediment into the sea where it formed the sea giant bearing zodiacal impressions; (3) the three cosmic purposes of the sea giant — purifying the sphere, fettering the zodiac powers, and gathering all elements. Every mapping stays within the cosmic system. The craftsman analogy in paragraph 6 serves cosmic explanation, not contemporary application. This is one of the purest substrate chapters in the corpus.*

After the Living Spirit constructed things and the worlds, he came up and set himself upon this earth. He gave a sign to the King of Glory, who turned the three wheels and caused the wheels that are found [ ... ] up above all the mountains; he spread forth the wind to them, he breathed on them, he stirred them up, he washed the earth with the waters. And the waste and the accretion of the three vessels, they of water and of Darkness and of fire, which had been discharged upon the earth, he gathered them and deposited them in the sea that is within the walls and the vessels; because of this sea waters are salty. For they have received salt and bitterness from the washing out and cleansing of the three vessels.

When the Father of Life came, rising in his ascent to the heights, he discharged the wheel of the stars; he washed it out and loosened all the sediment that was in it. He cast it down to this great inner sea like [ ... ] of the mountain. And that waste, the sediment of the washing out of the wheel, he cast it down and mixed it in with the sea waters. Its own thought collected it together; and the thought gathered the waste together in this way. It made it fast and fixed it [ ... ]

The sea giant had stamped upon him the seal of the seconds and the hours, the seal of the days and months and years, the impression of the stars and the signs of the zodiac [ ... ] He came into existence from them and was discharged from their power and cast into the sea. The images and the seals and aspects and doctrines and counsels of [ ... ] were sealed upon the body of that giant, because he is the residue of them all. Consequently, each star that will shine, and each sign of the zodiac that will turn: of one he shall be its inducement, and of another its confirmation.

He was discharged into the sea like this, the way a cloud might discharge rainwater into the midst of the sea. They compelled him totally through the action of the power and the helmsmanship of the angels. He was swept down to the mill, the place where he was constructed in the setting up of the universe; and the sea also conceived him [ ... ] for its part [ ... ] it being his chief sculptor and chief craftsman. It sculpted him in a likeness, he being changed more than the other residue of the sea bodies.

This matter is comparable with a craftsman who might take the stamped impressions of many coins and cast them on the fire, and mould them [to a single] image. These thousands of impressions and thousands of shapes [are perfected] and make a single body [ ... ]

⟨p.115⟩
This is also the case with the sea giant, who too was moulded and sculpted by the power of the lust inside him from many doctrines and counsels that belong to the residue of the wheel of the sphere.

The archetype of the giant was constructed for three things.

The first: That the sphere would be relieved and purified of the burden present in it. In the same way that the ten firmaments were purified and relieved of the burden of the three vessels; so was it also done to the sphere, that it too might be relieved and purified of the burden and the waste inside it.

The second: That he might add a {fetter} and a prison to the wheel and the signs of the zodiac that are bound to it, so that when they wish to go up above, you can find the sea giant dragging them back as by an iron {fetter}, and they are unable to go up; and when the powers of the wheel would take a decision on a guileful thing, and it enters their mind to come down to the earth, they are not able to descend because of this stake that is nailed to the wheel upon the {} of the firmaments. This is one. However, this other one, because of its sleepless watchers that are set over it, it is turned all the time.

The third thing: That the wind and the water and the fire that gush from the circuit of the sea, and the rivers that flow upon the face of the earth discharging into the sea, and the rainwaters that come to ground, and the waste and rebirth that come down to it; that sea will be a hostel and gathering place for all of these.

⟨p.116⟩
As for all the power and the life that comes down to the sea, and in the breath that the sea giant will breathe and draw it into him that he might disturb the sea [ ... a] fullness and a [ ... ] becomes between his movement [ ... ] purify; but the sea [ ... ]

**Temporal observations:**
- ¶1: Pure formulaic dialogue frame opening. 'Once again the enlightener speaks to his disciples' — stereotyped attribution formula with no cosmological content.
- ¶2: Systematic cosmological process: Living Spirit → King of Glory → three wheels → washing earth → waste deposited in sea. Every entity and mapping is cosmic. The explanation of salt/bitterness is a classic correspondential teaching — physical quality mapped to cosmic process. High substrate vocabulary (13.24).
- ¶3: Cosmic process continues: Father of Life purifies the stellar sphere by casting sediment into the sea. The 'thought' (of the waste matter) as an active agent collecting and fixing — this is the evil active thought that forms the sea giant. Purely cosmological, both sides internal to the system.
- ¶4: Zodiac-body correspondence teaching — the sea giant's body bears seals of temporal cycles (seconds, hours, days, months, years) and stellar/zodiacal impressions. This is the correspondential substrate at its purest: cosmic entity → temporal-astronomical correspondences. The 'inducement' and 'confirmation' describe the giant's functional relationship to stellar/zodiacal powers.
- ¶5: Cosmological process: the giant's formation described through cosmic mechanics — 'helmsmanship of the angels', 'the mill' as the construction site, the sea as sculptor/craftsman. The cloud-rain simile is a natural illustration of cosmic process, not an application. 'Sea bodies' implies a class of cosmic entities formed in the sea.
- ¶6: Teaching analogy explaining cosmic formation. The craftsman-coins simile illustrates how multiple stellar/zodiacal impressions were fused into the single body of the sea giant. Both sides of the analogy serve cosmic explanation — no reach into contemporary institutions. Zero substrate vocabulary score reflects absence of cosmic proper nouns, but the function is purely explanatory within the cosmic system. Integral to the teaching sequence (paragraph 7 explicitly ties it back).
- ¶7: Highest substrate score in chapter (27.5). Ties the craftsman analogy back to the cosmic entity. 'Lust' as active formative power, 'doctrines and counsels' as stellar properties, 'residue of the wheel of the sphere' — all cosmic.
- ¶8: Enumeration header introducing the three-fold cosmic purpose of the sea giant. This is a structural teaching marker — it defines the framework for the following enumerated teaching (paragraphs 9-11). Brief but integral to the substrate structure.
- ¶9: First enumerated purpose. Systematic cosmic process: purification of the sphere, paralleled with purification of the ten firmaments from the three vessels. High substrate vocabulary (18.64). Classic degree-mapping: firmaments/three vessels paralleled with sphere/waste.
- ¶10: Second enumerated purpose. The sea giant as cosmic fetter binding zodiacal powers — preventing both ascent and descent. 'Iron fetter' is a metal-function correspondence. 'Stake nailed to the wheel', 'sleepless watchers' — all cosmic functional entities. The 'you can find' is a didactic address mode within the teaching, not editorial application. Two restraining mechanisms: the giant below, the watchers above.
- ¶11: Third enumerated purpose. The sea as cosmic gathering vessel for elements — wind, water, fire, rivers, rain, waste. All entities are cosmic/elemental. 'Hostel and gathering place' describes cosmic function. Zero substrate vocabulary score despite being clearly substrate — the vocabulary counter misses this because it uses natural-world terms (rivers, rain) rather than cosmic proper nouns, but the function is entirely cosmological.
- ¶12: Heavily lacunose closing paragraph. What survives describes the sea giant breathing in life/power, and a purification process. Despite extensive lacunae, the surviving vocabulary is consistently cosmological — 'power', 'life', 'breath', 'purify'. The teaching appears to have described how life entering the sea is purified through the giant's activity, consistent with Gardner's synopsis.

## Chapter 45
### Concerning the Vessels

*Remarkably pure substrate chapter. Teaching purity 1.000 with zero editorial fatigue. The entire chapter follows a single systematic pattern — "Before X was discharged/born/rebelled, a place was prepared for it" — iterating through cosmic entities and their pre-prepared containment places. Both sides of every mapping stay within the cosmic-architectural system: vessels→ditches, sea giant→great sea, waste→gehennas, rebels→hostels, abortions→gathering-place, watchers→prison, giants' children→cities. No editorial bridge, institutional, or exhortation vocabulary detected anywhere. This chapter appears to preserve a nearly intact substrate teaching sequence about cosmic foreknowledge and containment architecture. The enumeration structure (each paragraph = one cosmic entity and its prepared place) is characteristic of the oldest systematic teaching layer.*

Understand also this other (matter): Before these vessels were discharged and swept down from heaven, first three ditches were fashioned and constructed for them, in which they would be bound and fettered.

Before the sea giant was discharged from the sphere, the Father of Life constructed a place for [him i]n this great sea that is within the walls, whereon the ships sail, and where the fullness shall [ ... ... ]

Also, before the [ ... ] was displayed to the worlds [ ... ] the earth [ ... ... f]ell to the sea and its thought shaped it, the Father of Life constructed a place for it in [the ... ... ] below the four mountains, above the mountains of the heavens above [ ... ] that [ ... ] a great plain it digs in Matter in the midst of the worlds. When the Adamant of Light was sent to it, he hastened after it to that place, smote it and threw it between mountains in the place made ready for it [ ... ] is set fast upon it till the end of the universe.

Before the waste and dirt of Darkness were swept out from creation, gehennas were constructed in which the waste might be covered till the dissolution of the universe.

Before the rebels rose up, they whose corps rebelled in the ruling-power above and below, they [ ... ] outside, and also the tyrants in whose heart it lay [to ty]rannise amongst the murk and the clouds and winds and spirits and storms; for them were constructed in the great outer sea seven hostels, places whence they are thrown into the depths and the darkness [ ... ]

Also, before the abortions [fell ... ...] to the earth, there was set apart [ ... ]: they are gathered in and dwell therein.

Again, before the watchers rebelled and came down from heaven, a prison was fashioned and constructed for them in the depths of the earth, below the mountains.

Before the children of the giants were born, they who had [no] knowledge of righteousness in them nor divinity, thirty-six cities were assigned and constructed for them wherein the children of [the giants would] live; they who would come to beget from each other, they who shall spend ten hundred years alive.

**Temporal observations:**
- ¶1: Opening transition 'Understand also this other (matter):' is a pedagogical marker within the teaching tradition, not a dialogue frame attribution. No honorifics, no Mani attribution, no 'once again' formula. The imperative voice ('understand') belongs to systematic instruction. Initiates the systematic enumeration pattern: Before X → Y was prepared.
- ¶2: Pure cosmic-architectural mapping: sea giant (cosmic entity) → great sea within the walls (cosmic geography). 'The sphere', 'the walls', 'the ships' are cosmic-architectural terms. High substrate score (17.5). The lacuna at end likely continued the cosmic description.
- ¶3: Dense cosmic geography: four mountains, mountains of the heavens, great plain in Matter, midst of the worlds. The Adamant of Light is a cosmic agent performing a cosmic function — smiting and imprisoning a cosmic entity in a prepared cosmic place. Heavy lacunae but all recoverable content is systematic cosmic process description. Both sides of every mapping stay within the cosmic system.
- ¶4: Concise iteration of the pattern: waste/dirt of Darkness (cosmic substance) → gehennas (cosmic containment). 'Gehennas' here functions as cosmic architecture, not moral/eschatological application. 'Dissolution of the universe' is cosmic temporal horizon. Capitalizing Darkness as it functions as a cosmic domain.
- ¶5: Cosmic rebellion mapped to cosmic containment. 'Rebels', 'corps', 'ruling-power above and below', 'tyrants', 'murk', 'clouds and winds and spirits and storms' — all cosmic entities/phenomena. 'Seven hostels' in the 'great outer sea' — cosmic geography with numerical specificity characteristic of substrate enumeration. Both sides entirely within the cosmic system.
- ¶6: Brief iteration — 'abortions' are cosmic entities (demonic offspring) in Manichaean cosmology, not human abortions. Their prepared place is lost in lacuna. The pattern 'Before X... a place was set apart' continues the systematic enumeration.
- ¶7: Watchers tradition (Enochic) integrated into the cosmic containment schema. 'Again' is an enumeration marker continuing the list. Watchers (cosmic beings) → prison in the depths of the earth below the mountains (cosmic geography). Both sides stay within the cosmic system. The Watcher tradition itself may be very old substrate shared with Enochic literature.
- ¶8: Final iteration: giants' children (cosmic beings) → thirty-six cities (cosmic geography). 'Thirty-six' is precise numerical specification characteristic of substrate systematization. 'Ten hundred years' is cosmic temporal measure. 'Knowledge of righteousness... nor divinity' describes what these beings ARE within the cosmic system — not moral exhortation. The enumeration pattern closes here, completing the catalogue of prepared containments.

## Chapter 46
### Concerning the Ambassador

*A highly systematic substrate teaching about cosmic pre-ordination/foreknowledge of the light. Seven moments follow a uniform pattern: 'Before [condition], [cosmic agent] was [prepared].' The structure is self-evidently complete and architectonic. Only ¶1 (dialogue frame) and ¶5 (editorial bridge inserting 'the sects' into the cosmic pattern) fall outside the oldest layer. Teaching purity is very high (0.913). The moderate institutional drift in the second half is minimal, concentrated in ¶5. The chapter preserves a remarkably intact substrate teaching about emanation sequence and cosmic determinism.*

Before night and day were differentiated in the universe, the Third [Am]bassador was summoned. And afterwards, he was sent to this void and outer waste that he might purify the light, and separate night and day from each other.

⟨p.117⟩ Before the light was released and freed and ascended through this zone, the Father of Life made beautiful for it the ships of light. He constructed them so that it might be purified in them, and be sculpted and fashioned in good order.

[Bef]ore Sin had multiplied and had made a kingdom in the flesh, before the acquitted and the condemn[ed] were set apart from each other, the Judge [w]as sent forth and revealed. His throne was created and placed [i]n the living atmosphere, so that he would be a just Judge, so that there the righteous and the sinners would be set apart and tested and separated from each other.

Again, [befo]re mankind had been engendered, some were named ['rich' and] others 'poor' on earth [ ... ] upon them. ⟨p.118⟩ Before they die, they who will die are marked out by the stars and the signs of the zodiac in the sphere. They are appointed for them; in them are their births. And their root is bound up with their zodiacal signs; and they are compelled by them and brought to an equal judgement in accordance with their deeds and their sins.

Again, before the universe is dissolved, [and the] entire earth, the destroyers were appointed and instructed for its dissolution.

And before the ne[w] aeon was [built and] made be[auti]ful and marked out, [the] Great Builder [w]as summoned, the first architect and the angels who are with him, who would build and make beautiful the new aeon.

**Temporal observations:**
- ¶1: Pure formulaic session opening: 'Once again, hear this other lesson that I would teach you!' First-person address to audience with no cosmological content. Standard compilation frame marker.
- ¶2: 'Know that' is a borderline case — it exists in Persian pedagogical tradition but is an audience-address formula. The cosmological content is pure substrate: Ambassador as cosmic agent, night/day differentiation, purification of light, void/outer waste as cosmic geography. Both sides of every mapping stay within the cosmic system.
- ¶3: Pure substrate. Father of Life → cosmic agent. Ships of light → cosmic vessels. Zone → cosmic geography. Light purification, sculpting, fashioning → cosmic process vocabulary. High substrate score (13.64). Both sides internal to cosmic system. The vocabulary of crafting/construction (sculpted, fashioned, constructed) maps emanation as architectural act.
- ¶4: Pure substrate. Sin personified as cosmic agent ('had multiplied and had made a kingdom') — capitalized per extraction rules. Judge → cosmic agent. Throne → cosmic seat. Living atmosphere → cosmic geography. The separating/testing function is cosmic process, not ethical exhortation. 'Acquitted and condemned' and 'righteous and sinners' function as cosmic categories (light/dark mixture being separated), not contemporary moral application.
- ¶5: Editorial extension mimicking the substrate 'Before X, Y was prepared' pattern but reaching outside the cosmic system. 'The sects' are contemporary religious groups in the editor's world — the ONLY paragraph where one side of the mapping leaves the cosmic architecture. 'The blessed Christ' uses honorific ('blessed') absent from the impersonal substrate voice. Institutional vocabulary score (6.45) confirms. If removed, the seven-moment teaching remains structurally complete with six cosmic stages (Ambassador → ships → Judge → stars/zodiac → destroyers → Builder).
- ¶6: Pure substrate. Astral determinism teaching — stars, zodiac, sphere are cosmic geography. 'Their root is bound up with their zodiacal signs' is a classic body-cosmos correspondence (root/zodiac binding formula). 'Rich'/'poor' function as cosmic fate categories determined by astral configuration, not social commentary. 'In accordance with their deeds and their sins' is cosmic mechanism (karmic process within the astral system), not moral exhortation. Lacuna [...] preserved.
- ¶7: Pure substrate. Cosmic eschatology — destroyers as cosmic agents appointed for dissolution of the universe. Both sides internal to cosmic system. Brief but structurally essential as the penultimate stage in the pre-ordination sequence.
- ¶8: Pure substrate with highest substrate score in chapter (21.62). Great Builder / first architect → cosmic agents. New aeon → cosmic geography/eschatology. 'Build and make beautiful' echoes the crafting vocabulary of ¶3 (Father of Life 'made beautiful' the ships). The structural closure: from pre-cosmic summoning (¶2, Ambassador) to post-cosmic construction (¶8, Builder). Multiple restorations from manuscript damage preserved.

## Chapter 47
### Concerning the Four great Things

*An exceptionally pure substrate chapter. The entire teaching — the four-fold distribution of the ruling-power (ten firmaments, eight earths, walls/vessels, stellar sphere), the binding of cosmic leaders to the wheel, and the king-kingdom structural correspondence — stays within the cosmic system throughout. No institutional, editorial bridge, or exhortation vocabulary detected. The only editorial material is the dialogue frame opening formula in P1 and the first-person closure in P9. The seam flag on P9 is a false positive: the bridge connective 'Now, just as' returns the king analogy to cosmic application rather than bridging to institutional content. Teaching purity 0.921 is confirmed by close reading.*

The wheel of the stars is a great mighty thing! Also, it is one among the four great things; since the whole ruling-power is assigned to four parts.

The first part is the powers who occur in the ten firmaments that exist above the wheel, and the ruling-power that is bound in them, as it is imprisoned within them in the heavens that are above.

The second part is the eight earths that are below; the four that are composite, and the four places of darkness. The second part extends from this earth, upon which mankind walks, towards the underside.

The third part is the walls and the vessels that surround the worlds. They are the four mountains and the three vessels; with what is in them, and the ruling-power that is trapped within them, and the powers who are masters over them.

The fourth part is the sphere of the stars that is appointed with great diligence, as it is ordered in a [ ... m]any [ ... ]; that of the Father of Life, [and that of the Mother of] the living.

⟨p.119⟩ Its [ ... ] very [ ... ] with diligence, because of the great and cruel powers, and the titanic figures, [who are] in it; they being suspended from it. So, all the first-born and the leaders of all the rulers, they who were existing in the three parts, these were garnered. They were collected together, seized, bound and assigned on this wheel. Each was apportioned by these three parts. They were distributed, entwined, and weighted against their companions of the same size.

Still, for its part, the wheel is equal to them in its strength; the powers of the enmity who are bound on it. Indeed, they were suspended on the circling wheel because of their cruelty; so that, due to the wind and the motion in which they go round, they comprehend not the place wherein they are set. For if they had been bound in the heavens, the heavens would not have had the strength to support them. Again, if they had been assigned on the earths, on the fixtures that are below, they also would not have had the strength to support them. Rather, they were suspended from the wheel of the circling stars; so that the power and the light that should come up from below can be received, and given to the heavenly ones above them. Conversely, whatever will fall from above, whether cold or hot, the wheel of the sphere will also take it and cast it out upon the entire earth, upon all the wood.

Behold, the ordinance of the wheel is in this form. For the powers who are entwined on the wheel are like this: a king mastering his kingdom by this mighty legionary force of all the leaders of the kingdom; and with all the armour of the soldiery that is collected together for him; and also with the mass of the property of the kingdom; and the beautiful lustful aspect of the women who are collected together for him; together with [ ... ] property [ ... ] and those cattle [ ... ] that have been distributed about among his retinue. Yet, if you ask about that king, he is matched with all his retinue. His retinue, for its part, is matched against his entire kingdom. Judgement shall come forth from him, vindication and condemnation, to his entire kingdom; by his wisdom, skill and strength.

Now, just as for this king, who is matched against the measure of his entire kingdom by his wisdom, skill and strength; so also is it for the powers who are bound on the sphere as compared to the ruling-power, and the powers who exist in the entire zone. Like the king towards his retinue; so too is it that this totality is gathered to the sphere, to seven leaders, to twelve signs of the zodiac. As all the cities are gathered to the king, again they gather to the leaders of the universe. All that is done in the universe above and below: the battles and the disorders and the captivity and the hunger and the lust and the property; so shall they increase and diminish through these leaders. They are the motivaters of the whole created order.

**Temporal observations:**
- ¶1: Opening formula is standard dialogue frame. The substantive content — wheel of stars as one of four great things, ruling-power assigned to four parts — sets up the chapter's four-fold enumeration structure. This is the teaching premise.
- ¶2: First element of four-fold enumeration. Systematic cosmic geography: ten firmaments above the wheel, ruling-power imprisoned within. Both sides of the mapping are internal to the cosmic system. High substrate score (29.73) confirmed.
- ¶3: Second element of enumeration. Eight earths subdivided into four composite and four dark. 'Upon which mankind walks' is a spatial reference marker, not editorial application — it locates the boundary within the cosmic structure. Substrate score registers 0.0 because tracked vocabulary terms are absent, but content is entirely cosmological.
- ¶4: Third element of enumeration. Cosmic architecture: walls, vessels, four mountains, three vessels. The 'powers who are masters over them' maps cosmic hierarchy. All internal to the system.
- ¶5: Fourth and final element of enumeration. Lacunae in the middle obscure some content. Father of Life and Mother of the living are cosmic agents within the emanation hierarchy. The four-fold structure is now complete.
- ¶6: Cosmic process description: how leaders from the three parts were gathered and bound on the wheel. Impersonal, systematic, process-oriented voice. 'Distributed, entwined, and weighted' describes the cosmic mechanics of binding. The 'titanic figures' is a striking term — possible Greek-influenced cosmological vocabulary in the Coptic translation.
- ¶7: Elaborate cosmological explanation of the wheel's mediating function. Three-part logical structure: (1) heavens couldn't bear them, (2) earths couldn't bear them, (3) therefore the wheel. Then the wheel's dual function: receiving light from below upward, distributing cold/hot from above downward. This is cosmic mechanics — the wheel as conduit between upper and lower domains. Both sides stay within the cosmic system throughout.
- ¶8: The king-kingdom analogy is a STRUCTURAL CORRESPONDENCE — ruler matched to totality of domain. This is not application to contemporary institutions but an archetypal mapping expressing the cosmic principle. No contemporary persons, institutions, or religious practices named. The king is a type, not a historical figure. 'Behold' is a common teaching introducer in the tradition. The principle — that ruler equals domain — is the same correspondential principle that governs the cosmic architecture being taught.
- ¶9: The seam flag is a false positive. 'Now, just as' bridges the king analogy BACK to the cosmic system, not outward to institutional content. Seven leaders (planets) and twelve zodiac signs are cosmic architecture. The content maps the structural correspondence from P8 onto the cosmic reality it was designed to illuminate. The only non-substrate material is the final first-person closure. The teaching itself — that the seven planetary leaders and twelve zodiac signs govern the entire created order — is foundational cosmological-correspondential content.

## Chapter 48
### Concerning the Conduits

*Overwhelmingly substrate chapter. The teaching describes three conduits (lihme) connecting heavenly powers to earthly domains, through which life ascends and waste descends. The entire cosmological architecture—conduits, roots, the wheel of stars, the Ambassador myth, cosmic geography—maps domain to domain without ever reaching into the contemporary world. No editorial bridge, institutional, or exhortation vocabulary detected at all. The only later material consists of thin dialogue frame formulas wrapping the teaching. The Persian dialogue tradition is strong here: the disciples' questions about zodiacal authority and conduit hierarchy reveal teaching structure. Heavy lacunae in the later paragraphs (22-24) but the surviving words are consistently cosmological.*

There are three conduits that exist through the entire zone, from the abyss to its heights.

The first conduit is the root of all the powers above, they that exist in all the heavens. They are cast off and fettered on the earths below; because the earth below is the 'stripping off', the garment and the body of the powers that are above the heavens.

For when the Father of Life constructed the heavenly worlds, he divested them of their bodies, [he] threw them to the earths below.

So, the upper worlds are of the soul and the spirit; but the worlds below are of the body and the carcass [...] ⟨p.121⟩ Now as for this, he [...] the powers of heaven; he sealed it on their bodies and their carcasses that are on the earths, so that when life comes up from the fullness of these earths, it can be entirely drawn up to the heights in the roots of what is heavenly, that are fastened in their bodies. And also, all life can be purified in that place.

However, the waste that will be swept from the purified one who ascends to the heights will be let fall to the ground through the conduits of these conduits, and will be poured down [...] and thrown to the underside.

This is the first conduit, which comes from the powers of the heavens to their bodies and their carcasses that are upon the earth; also, from their bodies and [their] carcasses that are on the earths to the powers of the heavens.

The second conduit is the one that comes from the temples and dwellings and cities that exist in the heavens down to the ground, to the five shapes of tree that grow upon the earth. The life comes up from the trees to the temples and cities. Life too, and the residue of the heavenly ones, comes down to the trees in a conduit.

And the third conduit is all the powers and householders who dwell in all the firmaments, whose roots come down from them to the five worlds of flesh that creep upon the earth, being fixed to one another. Now, the power and the life that shall be gathered in from the worlds of flesh are dispersed among them in various shapes. The powers above shall draw them to the upper side through conduits.

[The was]te too, and the lust and the evil-doing and the anger that will be greater in the powers of [heave]n, shall be poured to the ground through their various conduits. They shall be discharged upon mankind and the other remaining animals. When what is heavenly will wash the waste and the stench and the poison down on the creations of the flesh below, in their turn the creations shall be greater in lust and anger and evil-doing against each other through the action of their fathers who are on high.

Still, look at the wheel of the stars! It has no [root in this earth], but its roots are bound in the totality. The wheel of stars, then, receives life through the power and the life that ascend from the earths through conduits and the firmaments above. It (also) takes life from those conduits that receive up to the firmaments and the heavenly worlds above it, it takes life out from that place.

In that the wheel has no root in this earth, from where did the stars and signs of the zodiac find this authority? They became masters over these five fleshes and five trees. Every thing, if it can be reduced from the earth, the heavenly powers above draw it through a conduit. Also, every thing that a person will attain, whether it be wealth that comes into his possession or poverty that will accrue to him, or his sickness and health; he attains it through the signs of the zodiac and the star under which he shall be born.

the wheel has no root therein. It is set aright in its position. For when the Living Spirit, the Father of Life, constructed the heavens and the firmaments above, he bound their roots into the earths below; in their carcasses that he had stripped from them. Yet, when he constructed the wheel, [he] did not bind its root into the earths, but he bound the root of the wheel by the sea giant.

And when [the] Ambassador came, he displayed his image and purified and brought the light out from all [the] heavens. Matter, however, which is present in all the rulers, spurted out against the image of the Ambassador. It went up in order to reach it, but reached it not. It dropped back and fell to the [under]side. Now, when it had fallen from above, it separated out into three parts.

The first part fell to the wheel; another part fell to the earth; an[other part fell to the se]a. ⟨p.123⟩ [By means] of the part that fell to the sphere, [the r]oot was bound in the trees and flesh. Therefore, because of this, they received authority and became masters in the five worlds of the flesh and the five worlds of the tree.

Through the nature that came down [upo]n the wheel the root was bound in the trees. Also, by means of the abortions that came to the ground, another root was bound in the fleshes.

Therefore, because of this, the stars and signs of the zodiac received authority over the trees and the fleshes: through the root of Sin that fell down; and [the ro]ot of the abortions that were aborted there, they fell to the ground.

However, the[se] other conduits that [the] Living Spirit has bound in the e[a]rths, they that the soul of the elements comes up through to the heavens; those (conduits) are not bound to the wheel. Rather, they are plundered from them by the powers on the wheel, that hang from it.

The first is the one that comes from the firmaments to the earths, and the one that comes from the wheel to the sea giant. That is only one. So, the other root is the one that comes [fr]om [the temples] to the trees. The third is bound by means of the abortions in the f[leshes]; also in the fleshes the fathers of the abortions who are in the heavens [...] are bound and twined, and the [y] that [...]

about the conduits, the one that is greater than its [partn]er [...] as more life and soul is drawn [...] than its partner.

great [...] the earth from which much life ascends [...] the Father of Life has drawn them. ⟨p.124⟩ He has fastened them from the powers [...] he has bound them in the earths. (These conduits) are the roots of the ent[ire] ruling-power that the life of the living soul is drawn up through, it coming up from the whole earth, from all the mountains, fields and islands.

After that first great root is the root of the trees, which is bound to the temples and the buildings above. For there also is much great life coming up from the tree and the entire Cross, being drawn up from them to the upper side like a great host.

What, however, is lesser [...] of their conduit [...] he takes up [...] the roots [...] the earths, they [...] the whole earth in all the worlds. Again, these of the trees are present in the whole earth, whether small or great; but the root of mankind [...] is not present in the whole earth, other than only this world of the south. Therefore, even now these fleshes do not make [...] in the entire world of the south [...] there is a small part of them in [...] of the upper side of the world of the south in [...] in number than the [...]

they are bound by their [roots ...] with one another. They stir one another, they draw life from one another through the action of the powers above, which stirs the nature that dwells in them all.

Now, when the Matter that is in them will be crushed and despoiled, then that Matter too shall [...] its members and overturn them and [...] each other [...] stir [...] ⟨p.125⟩

**Temporal observations:**
- ¶1: Frame formula 'Once again the enlightener speaks' wraps a concise cosmological proposition defining the three conduits and their scope (abyss to heights). The teaching statement is the kernel of the entire chapter.
- ¶2: Pure correspondential teaching. The earth IS the body/garment of the heavenly powers. Both sides of the mapping are cosmic domains. 'Stripping off' is technical cosmological vocabulary for the divestment process.
- ¶3: Cosmological process narrative. 'Father of Life' is a cosmic agent (the Living Spirit). Describes the construction event that established the conduit system.
- ¶4: Systematic degree-mapping: upper worlds = soul and spirit, lower worlds = body and carcass. This is classic correspondential substrate—the psychic/pneumatic vs. somatic/ptomatic distinction mapped onto cosmic geography. The conduit mechanism (life drawn up through roots) is pure cosmological process.
- ¶5: Describes the descending function of the conduit system. Purification process: life ascends, waste descends. Both directions stay within the cosmic architecture.
- ¶6: Summary of the first conduit. Bi-directional mapping: heavenly powers ↔ earthly bodies/carcasses. Pure cosmic system description.
- ¶7: Second conduit maps HEAVENLY temples/cities → five shapes of tree. 'Temples and dwellings and cities' are cosmic structures in the heavens, not earthly institutions. 'Five shapes of tree' is systematic five-element nomenclature applied to vegetation. Both sides cosmic.
- ¶8: Third conduit: firmament powers → five worlds of flesh. 'Householders' are cosmic rulers, not human inhabitants. 'Five worlds of flesh' is systematic five-element nomenclature. The dispersal and gathering mechanism is pure cosmic process.
- ¶9: Describes cosmic determinism through the descending conduit. 'Mankind' here is a category of the five worlds of flesh (cosmic taxonomy), not a contemporary audience. Lust, anger, evil-doing are cosmic qualities poured down from 'fathers who are on high'—heavenly powers. Both sides of the mapping remain cosmic. No exhortation or application.
- ¶10: Teaching about the wheel of stars and its unique rootedness. Unlike the three conduits, the wheel has no root in earth but draws life from the conduit system itself. 'Look at the wheel of the stars!' is pedagogical voice, not frame—it directs attention to a cosmic object.
- ¶11: Substantive cosmological question in the Persian pedagogical tradition. The paradox—the wheel has no earthly root yet controls earthly fate—defines the teaching problem that paragraphs 12-16 resolve. The question about zodiacal authority over five fleshes and five trees uses systematic nomenclature. The observation about wealth, poverty, sickness, health is cosmological determinism, not contemporary application.
- ¶12: Core teaching distinguishes the wheel's binding from the firmaments' binding. The wheel is bound to the sea giant, not to the earths. The Living Spirit's construction sequence is cosmic process narrative. 'Sea giant' is a cosmic entity (cf. chapter 44).
- ¶13: The Ambassador myth—a core cosmological narrative. The Ambassador displays his image, Matter in the rulers ejaculates upward, fails to reach the image, and falls in three parts. This is cosmic process description, not historical narrative. 'Ambassador' and 'Matter' function as cosmic agents.
- ¶14: Three-fold distribution of fallen Matter: wheel, earth, sea. The sphere's portion established the root binding zodiacal authority to vegetation and flesh. 'Five worlds of the flesh and the five worlds of the tree' is systematic five-element nomenclature. This resolves the paradox raised in paragraph 11.
- ¶15: Further specification of the binding mechanism: fallen nature → trees, abortions → fleshes. Two distinct root-binding events. 'Abortions' is technical cosmological vocabulary for the demonic spawn that fell to earth.
- ¶16: Seam flag is a false positive—the 'institutional vocabulary' field is empty. 'Therefore, because of this' is a logical connective summarizing the cosmic process of paragraphs 13-15. Content is entirely cosmological: zodiacal authority derived from the root of Sin and abortions. Both sides cosmic. Sin capitalized as personified cosmic agent.
- ¶17: Critical distinction: the three conduits are NOT bound to the wheel—they are plundered by the wheel's powers. The Living Spirit bound the conduits in the earths. 'Soul of the elements' is technical substrate vocabulary for the light trapped in matter.
- ¶18: Substantive recapitulation of the three conduits by the disciples—reveals the teaching structure. This is the Persian pedagogical pattern: disciples demonstrate comprehension by restating the system. The enumeration (first, other, third) and the cosmic mappings are substrate. Heavy lacunae at the end.
- ¶19: Substantive cosmological question about the hierarchy of conduits by volume of life drawn. This question defines the teaching topic for paragraphs 20-22. Fragment survives through lacunae but the structural content is clear.
- ¶20: The first conduit is greatest because it is bound in all the earths. 'Mountains, fields and islands' is cosmic geography—all terrestrial domains from which life ascends. The Father of Life is a cosmic agent. Heavy lacunae but surviving words are consistently cosmological.
- ¶21: Second conduit ranked: trees bound to heavenly temples/buildings. 'The entire Cross' is the Cross of Light—a cosmic entity referring to the light trapped in all vegetation across the earth, not the Christian cross. The image of life 'drawn up like a great host' describes cosmic process.
- ¶22: Third conduit is least because flesh only covers part of the world. 'World of the south' is cosmic geography—the inhabited portion of earth in the Manichaean cosmological scheme. Trees cover the whole earth but flesh only a small part. Despite heavy lacunae, every surviving word describes cosmic spatial distribution.
- ¶23: Cosmological summary of the conduit system's mutual interdependence. The three conduits are bound together and exchange life through the action of heavenly powers. 'The nature that dwells in them all' is substrate vocabulary for the cosmic mixture.
- ¶24: Eschatological cosmological process—the final crushing and despoiling of Matter. Despite heavy lacunae, surviving words describe cosmic process. Matter is a personified cosmic agent. This appears to be the chapter's conclusion describing the end-state of the conduit system.

## Chapter 49
### Concerning the Wheel and the Conduits

*A remarkably clean, short teaching chapter. The oldest substrate layer is overwhelmingly dominant. The only later material is minimal dialogue frame formulas in ¶1 and ¶2. No editorial bridge, no institutional content, no exhortation. The teaching is a systematic cosmological-process explanation: the conduits (extending from heavens to earths) are spiritual/pneumatic, therefore the wheel of stars passes through them without cutting or entangling — illustrated by ship-through-water and bird-through-air analogies. Both sides of every mapping stay within the cosmic/natural system. The negative editorial fatigue score (-1.54) with substrate increasing in the second half confirms the chapter is substrate-dominant throughout.*

How does the wheel circle over the conduits, which extend from the heavens to the earths, and it not cut them? Or why shall the conduits not get entangled in with the wheel as it circles and drives [ ... through the u]niverse?

The reason that the conduits shall not be cut, for they are not cut, is because they are spiritual. Thus the conduits are like the waters through which ships sail. You shall find the prow that is in position in the front part of the ship divides the waters, casting them back and forth. So, when it parts the waters and that ship opens up the water with its cleaving prow, immediately and without delay the waters mingle with each other again behind the ship; and the path of the ship shall not be discernible in the midst of the waters.

Just as when a bird flies up in the air, for its path is not visible in the air, and in the way of mankind, in the sun within the conduits. They also shall not be tangled up in them, and neither shall you find their path through the air known.

This is also the case with the wheel. It turns through the conduits and the air. The wheel shall not cut the conduits, nor are the conduits themselves entangled in the wheel.

**Temporal observations:**
- ¶1: The question itself is substantive and cosmological: it names the wheel, the conduits, heavens, earths, and defines the problem the teaching will address. This is the Persian pedagogical tradition — the question structures the teaching. Lacuna preserved.
- ¶2: The core teaching states the principle (conduits are spiritual, therefore cannot be cut) and then illustrates with a detailed ship-through-water analogy. Both sides of the mapping stay within the cosmic/natural system: spiritual conduits ↔ water, wheel ↔ ship. The analogy is process-oriented and impersonal — classic substrate voice. The detail about the prow and water closing behind suggests careful pedagogical elaboration, likely part of the original teaching tradition.
- ¶3: Continues the analogical teaching with no frame markers. Bird-through-air parallel to ship-through-water: both illustrate the spiritual/pneumatic principle. The text is somewhat garbled ('in the way of mankind, in the sun within the conduits') — possibly translation difficulty or manuscript damage. The teaching voice remains impersonal and systematic.
- ¶4: Very high substrate density (37.5). This is the concluding statement that closes the teaching unit, restating the principle in direct cosmological terms: wheel, conduits, air. Pure process description — both sides internal to the cosmic system. No editorial voice whatsoever.

## Chapter 50
### Concerning these Names: God, Rich One, and Angel; who they are.

*A remarkably clean substrate chapter. The teaching is a systematic three-fold emanation hierarchy (gods, rich ones, angels) mapped across three domains: eternal archetypes, cosmic agents in the contest, and eschatological households. Both sides of every mapping stay within the cosmic system throughout. The only non-substrate material is dialogue frame attribution (¶2 fully, and frame formulas in ¶1, ¶3, ¶7, ¶10). No editorial bridge, no exhortation, no institutional extension. The seam flag on ¶10 is a false positive — the third application maps to cosmic households (Great Spirit, Beloved of the Lights, Ambassador), not to institutional structures. The order shift in the third application (god→angel→rich instead of god→rich→angel) may reflect intentional mapping to three emanation series (descent, creation, redemption). Teaching purity is high and editorial fatigue is minimal.*

This one, a name is given to him in the world. For people pronounce it with their mouths: 'god', 'rich one', and 'angel'. Who are they? Who are the gods? Or who are the rich ones? Or who are the angels? Who are these [three] archetypes?

There are these three archetypes [in the land of light, and there is no measure to] them!

So, the ones who are called 'god', are the gods whom the Father has summoned from himself. He has established them after the likeness of his greatness.

Conversely, these who are named 'rich [ ... ]', are the evocations of the first rich gods of the Father; because, when the Father had summoned (the first), they themselves summoned evocations. They called them 'rich'.

They too, the rich ones, have summoned their evocations. They call them 'angel'. These are the three archetypes who occur in the land of light. There is no measure to apply to them!

Other persons who are named 'god' are the emanations who have come from the Father; the evocations of the Father whom he summoned forth. They came out to the contest and humiliated the enmity.

Conversely, the 'rich ones' are the evocations of these first living words. They too, the rich ones, have come and performed and fulfilled the pleasure of the greatness, in the worlds that are above and below.

On the other hand, the ones who are named the 'angels' are the evocations of the rich ones, who had come from the three living words. They have been sent in an embassy and an apostolate to this building. They have come to the entire divinity, which is established in silence and in hiddenness. And, also, they have come to all the souls who have been entangled in the enemy. They have brought them hope and confidence.

Again, they are called 'god', all the gods who belong to the household of the Great Spirit.

Conversely, the ones who are called 'angel', are all the angels who belong to the household of the Beloved of the Lights.

The ones who are called 'rich', are all the rich ones who belong to the household of the glorious Ambassador.

**Temporal observations:**
- ¶1: The substantive cosmological question defines the entire teaching structure: three names mapped to three archetypes. This is the teaching's own self-description and belongs to the substrate. The frame formula is standard editorial attribution.
- ¶2: Pure formulaic disciple question with no cosmological content. 'Tell us, our master, who they are!' adds no information beyond what ¶1 already established.
- ¶3: The teaching declaration situates the three archetypes in the land of light. The lacuna contains restoration. The 'no measure' formula is a recurrent substrate marker for the infinite nature of light-realm entities.
- ¶4: First element of the three-fold emanation hierarchy at the archetype level. Gods = direct emanation from the Father. 'Summoned from himself' is the evocation formula. Both sides of the mapping stay within the cosmic system.
- ¶5: Second element: rich ones = second-order evocations. The teaching explains the emanation chain: Father summons gods, gods summon evocations called 'rich'. The lacuna in 'rich [...]' is manuscript damage. 'Conversely' is a substrate structural transition marker.
- ¶6: Third element completing the archetype-level hierarchy: angels = third-order evocations. The closing statement ('These are the three archetypes who occur in the land of light') frames the first application as complete, a structural closure typical of substrate teaching.
- ¶7: Begins the second application: the three categories mapped to cosmic agents who entered the contest. Gods at this level = emanations who came forth to the cosmic conflict. 'Contest' and 'enmity' are cosmic-drama vocabulary. Both sides remain within the cosmic system.
- ¶8: Second element of the cosmic-agent level. Rich ones = evocations of the 'first living words' (the gods of ¶7). 'Worlds that are above and below' is systematic cosmic geography. 'Conversely' again serves as substrate structural marker.
- ¶9: Third element completing the cosmic-agent level. 'This building' = the cosmos (standard Manichaean cosmological term). 'Divinity established in silence and hiddenness' = the trapped light. 'Souls entangled in the enemy' = cosmic bondage. All referents stay within the cosmic system. The 'embassy and apostolate' vocabulary has institutional overtones but here describes cosmic function — the sending of angels to the mixed cosmos. The institutional score (5.19) is a vocabulary false positive.
- ¶10: Begins the third application: categories mapped to eschatological households. The seam flag fires due to 'Once again the enlightener speaks' + pattern extension, but the content maps gods→Great Spirit's household — both sides remain within the cosmic system. The Great Spirit is a cosmic figure (first emanation series), not an institutional entity. False positive seam detection.
- ¶11: Second element of the household-level mapping. Note the order shift: god→angel→rich instead of the earlier god→rich→angel. The Beloved of the Lights belongs to the creation series of emanations. Cosmos→cosmos mapping intact.
- ¶12: Third element completing the household-level mapping. The Ambassador belongs to the redemption series. The chapter closes cleanly with the third iteration complete — no editorial tail, no exhortation. The reordering across the three applications (god-rich-angel at archetype level, god-rich-angel at cosmic level, god-angel-rich at household level) may reflect deliberate mapping to different emanation series.

## Chapter 51
### Concerning the First Man

*Remarkably clean substrate chapter. Teaching purity 1.000 with zero editorial fatigue and zero scores for editorial_bridge, institutional, or exhortation across all paragraphs. The entire chapter (minus the one-line dialogue frame opening) is systematic cosmological teaching about the First Man's five garments (fire, wind, water, light, air) and their cosmic roles in the battle with Darkness. Both sides of every mapping stay within the cosmic system throughout. The five-element nomenclature and the process of mixture/wounding are core substrate features. The text is heavily lacunose but the teaching structure is clear.*

When the [First] Man came forth against the ene[my ...] ⟨p.127⟩ garment [...] the Darkness with the garment of [of] living fire. With that garment he withered the tumescence (?) of the enemy; he brought down i[t]s foolery and its vanity.

Now, this one alone was sufficient for them. [Y]et, si[n]ce the living fire was weakened, because of this he added [to it another fou]r garments: that of the wind, that of the water, that of the light, and that of the a[i]r. And this, (why) has he given the four? He gave them s[o] th[a]t they would be helpe[r]s to t[h]at god, wh[ich i]s the living fire; so that [...] not with [in] the living f[ire], and penetrate it; and [the po]ison and the [enmi]ty cut it off.

While the fire received a bl[ow and] a wound in its body; yet still, [...] all the powers of Da[rk]ness because of it [...] It [...] necessity [...] brothers, to cause them to receive a blow and a woun[d to]o. The power of the en/emy mixed in with them. Rath[er, ex]cept [the air]. The Darkness did not mix in with it, nor was the burning of the enemy able to penet/rate it.

Never-theless, even the one that remained behind without the wound, which is the air, it stripped off garment and power. It gave it to its [fo]ur brothers in its place; so that by that [ga]rment of the living air they would become strong, and [...] profit, and endure, namely its other four brothers. And it [be]came for them [...] life.

**Temporal observations:**
- ¶1: Pure formulaic attribution: 'Once again our light father speaks:' — no cosmological content whatsoever. Standard dialogue frame opener.
- ¶2: Cosmic process description: the First Man deploys the garment of living fire against Darkness. Both sides internal to the cosmic system — cosmic agent vs. cosmic adversary, cosmic element as weapon. Heavily lacunose but the substrate structure is clear.
- ¶3: Systematic five-element enumeration: fire already deployed, then the four additional garments (wind, water, light, air) given as helpers. The rhetorical question 'And this, (why) has he given the four?' is a pedagogical device within the teaching tradition — it structures the explanation, not an editorial insertion. The five-element nomenclature is a core substrate marker.
- ¶4: Describes the cosmic process of mixture — fire wounded, the other elements wounded and mixed with the enemy's power, except air which remained unmixed. 'Brothers' here is cosmological kinship among the five elements, not audience address. The mixture process and the exception of air are core cosmological-process teaching. Heavily lacunose but the teaching survives.
- ¶5: Completes the cosmic process: the unwounded air sacrificially gives its garment to the wounded four, strengthening them. This is systematic cosmic-process teaching — the air's unique role in the five-element structure. The kinship language ('brothers') is cosmological, describing internal relations among the five elements. The pattern (one giving to the four) is structural substrate.
